*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluCiteChecker是一个轻量级工具包，用于检测科学论文中的幻觉引用（即指向不存在文献的虚假引用），可在标准笔记本电脑上数秒内完成验证，支持离线CPU运行，并通过PyPI分发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手在学术写作中的广泛应用导致了幻觉引用（不存在的虚假引用）的出现，这类引用损害论文可信度并给审稿人和作者带来额外验证负担，亟需自动化检测工具。

Method: 将幻觉引用检测形式化为NLP任务，开发了轻量级工具包HalluCiteChecker，能够在标准笔记本电脑上以CPU模式离线运行，数秒内完成验证。

Result: 提供了一个可实际部署的检测工具包，代码在GitHub上以Apache 2.0协议开源，可通过PyPI安装，并有演示视频可供参考。

Conclusion: HalluCiteChecker有望减轻审稿人工作负担，支持组织者进行系统性的预审和出版检查，为学术出版质量控制提供实用基础。

Abstract: We introduce HalluCiteChecker, a toolkit for detecting and verifying hallucinated citations in scientific papers. While AI assistant technologies have transformed the academic writing process, including citation recommendation, they have also led to the emergence of hallucinated citations that do not correspond to any existing work. Such citations not only undermine the credibility of scientific papers but also impose an additional burden on reviewers and authors, who must manually verify their validity during the review process. In this study, we formalize hallucinated citation detection as an NLP task and provide a corresponding toolkit as a practical foundation for addressing this problem. Our package is lightweight and can perform verification in seconds on a standard laptop. It can also be executed entirely offline and runs efficiently using only CPUs. We hope that HalluCiteChecker will help reduce reviewer workload and support organizers by enabling systematic pre-review and publication checks. Our code is released under the Apache 2.0 license on GitHub and is distributed as an installable package via PyPI. A demonstration video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [116] [What Kind of Language is Easy to Language-Model Under Curriculum Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26844)
*Nadine El-Naggar,Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Ted Briscoe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了课程学习（从简单句子开始训练）如何影响语言模型的归纳偏差，进而影响其对人类语言类型学倾向（如语序偏好）的复现能力。研究发现，课程学习显著改变了语言模型表现出来的归纳偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在何种条件下可以预测语言类型学倾向（如常见语序 vs. 罕见语序），特别是语言模型的学习偏差是否足以复现这些模式。此前研究关注模型本身的归纳偏差，但忽略了学习场景这一维度。

Method: 在已有语言模型探索（El-Naggar et al., 2025a,b）基础上，引入简单的课程学习变体（curriculum learning, CL），即从简单句子开始训练而非随机顺序输入，通过对比分析CL对模型归纳偏差的影响。

Result: 课程学习显著影响了语言模型所表现出的归纳偏差，即训练数据的呈现顺序会改变模型对语言类型学特征的偏好。

Conclusion: 学习场景（如课程学习）与语言模型的归纳偏差之间存在交互作用，在利用语言模型研究人类语言类型学倾向时，需考虑训练数据顺序这一额外维度。

Abstract: Many of the thousands of attested languages share common configurations of features, creating a spectrum from typologically very rare (e.g., object-verb-subject word order) or impossible languages to very common combinations of features (e.g., subject-object-verb word order). One central question is under what conditions such typological tendencies can be predicted, and specifically whether the learning bias of language models (LMs) is sufficient to reproduce such patterns. In this study, we add one dimensionality to such analysis -- the learning scenario for LMs -- to explore its interaction with the inductive bias of LMs. Specifically, as a first study, we examine the effect of curriculum learning (CL), as a developmentally motivated learning scenario, i.e., starting with simpler sentences rather than randomly-ordered input. We expand existing LM-based exploration (El-Naggar et al., 2025a,b) with a simple CL variant and find that CL substantially impacts the apparent inductive bias of LMs.

</details>


### [117] [MoRFI: Monotonic Sparse Autoencoder Feature Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26866)
*Dimitris Dimakopoulos,Shay B. Cohen,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制微调实验发现，在参数量不变的情况下引入新知识会破坏LLM沿残差流特定方向检索已有知识的能力，导致幻觉；并提出MoRFI方法识别与幻觉因果相关的稀疏自编码器潜变量，通过单潜变量干预可恢复知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知监督微调引入新知识会加剧幻觉，但背后的机制尚不明确。

Method: 对Llama 3.1 8B、Gemma 2 9B和Mistral 7B v03进行受控微调实验，控制新知识比例和训练轮次；利用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析残差流激活，提出MoRFI方法筛选与目标属性单调相关的潜变量。

Result: 逐步引入新知识会增加幻觉，且延长训练会放大该效应；发现存在一组因果相关的残差流方向，其被新知识干扰后导致知识检索失败；通过单潜变量干预可恢复被破坏的知识。

Conclusion: 新知识的微调会破坏模型沿特定残差流方向检索已有知识的能力，这是幻觉产生的关键机制；MoRFI可跨模型可靠地发现这些方向，并通过单潜变量干预实现知识恢复。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire most of their factual knowledge during the pre-training stage, through next token prediction. Subsequent stages of post-training often introduce new facts outwith the parametric knowledge, giving rise to hallucinations. While it has been demonstrated that supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on new knowledge may exacerbate the problem, the underlying mechanisms are still poorly understood. We conduct a controlled fine-tuning experiment, focusing on closed-book QA, and find latent directions that causally contribute to hallucinations. Specifically, we fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 2 9B and Mistral 7B v03 on seven distinct single QA datasets, controlling for the percentage of new knowledge and number of training epochs. By measuring performance on the test set, we validate that incrementally introducing new knowledge increases hallucinations, with the effect being more pronounced with prolonged training. We leverage pre-trained sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze residual stream activations across various checkpoints for each model and propose Monotonic Relationship Feature Identification (MoRFI) for capturing causally relevant latents. MoRFI filters SAE features that respond monotonically to controlled fine-tuning data mixtures of a target property. Our findings show that exposure to unknown facts disrupts the model's ability to retrieve stored knowledge along a set of directions in the residual stream. Our pipeline reliably discovers them across distinct models, recovering knowledge through single-latent interventions.

</details>


### [118] [HealthNLP_Retrievers at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Cascaded LLM Pipeline for Grounded Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26880)
*Md Biplob Hosen,Md Alomgeer Hussein,Md Akmol Masud,Omar Faruque,Tera L Reynolds,Lujie Karen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多阶段级联管道系统，结合Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型，用于患者门户中电子健康记录的接地问答任务。系统包含查询重构、证据评分、答案生成和答案-证据对齐四个模块，在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者门户虽提供电子健康记录的直接访问权限，但患者难以理解或处理其中复杂的临床信息。现有方法缺乏对患者问题的准确理解和对临床证据的可靠接地，亟需一种能够将患者问题与电子健康记录中相关证据有效连接的系统。

Method: 采用多阶段级联管道架构，集成四个模块：(1) 少样本查询重构单元，用于总结冗长患者查询；(2) 基于启发式的证据评分器，对临床句子排序以优先保证召回率；(3) 接地响应生成器，严格基于已识别证据合成专业级答案；(4) 高精度多对多对齐框架，将生成答案链接到支持的临床句子。底层使用Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型。

Result: 在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中，系统在问题解释赛道排名第1，答案生成赛道排名第5，证据识别赛道排名第7，答案-证据对齐赛道排名第9。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型集成到结构化多阶段管道中能够改善接地性、精确性和面向患者的健康沟通的专业质量。代码已公开在GitHub上以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Patient portals now give individuals direct access to their electronic health records (EHRs), yet access alone does not ensure patients understand or act on the complex clinical information contained in these records. The ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task addresses this challenge by focusing on grounded question answering over EHRs, and this paper presents the system developed by the HealthNLP_Retrievers team for this task. The proposed approach uses a multi-stage cascaded pipeline powered by the Gemini 2.5 Pro large language model to interpret patient-authored questions and retrieve relevant evidence from lengthy clinical notes. Our architecture comprises four integrated modules: (1) a few-shot query reformulation unit which summarizes verbose patient queries; (2) a heuristic-based evidence scorer which ranks clinical sentences to prioritize recall; (3) a grounded response generator which synthesizes professional-caliber answers restricted strictly to identified evidence; and (4) a high-precision many-to-many alignment framework which links generated answers to supporting clinical sentences. This cascaded approach achieved competitive results. Across the individual tracks, the system ranked 1st in question interpretation, 5th in answer generation, 7th in evidence identification, and 9th in answer-evidence alignment. These results show that integrating large language models within a structured multi-stage pipeline improves grounding, precision, and the professional quality of patient-oriented health communication. To support reproducibility, our source code is publicly available in our GitHub repository

</details>


### [119] [Select to Think: Unlocking SLM Potential with Local Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26940)
*Wenxuan Ye,Yangyang Zhang,Xueli An,Georg Carle,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对小语言模型推理能力不足的问题，本文提出SELECT TO THINK (S2T)方法，将大语言模型从开放生成转变为从小模型top-K候选中进行选择，并进一步蒸馏选择逻辑到小模型中，使其无需推理时依赖大模型即可自主重排序。实验表明，1.5B小模型的top-8候选能以95%命中率覆盖32B大模型的选择，S2T-LOCAL相比贪心解码平均提升24.1%，效果媲美8路自一致性采样同时保持单轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型计算效率高但推理能力不足，现有方法要么通过调用大模型生成纠正token导致高延迟和成本，要么因容量限制难以通过标准蒸馏准确模仿大模型的复杂生成分布。

Method: 提出S2T方法：1) 识别局部充分性，即在大模型与小模型分歧点，大模型偏好的token始终位于小模型top-K候选内；2) 将大模型角色从开放生成转变为从SLM候选集中选择，简化监督信号为离散候选排序；3) 蒸馏该选择逻辑到小模型（S2T-LOCAL），使其能自主重排序，无需推理时依赖大模型。

Result: 1.5B小模型的top-8候选以95%命中率覆盖32B大模型选择；S2T-LOCAL相比贪心解码平均提升24.1%，效果媲美8路自一致性采样同时保持单轨迹推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过将大模型从生成者转变为选择者，并蒸馏选择逻辑，小模型可以有效弥补推理能力差距，无需推理时调用大模型，实现了性能与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) offer computational efficiency for scalable deployment, yet they often fall short of the reasoning power exhibited by their larger counterparts (LLMs). To mitigate this gap, current approaches invoke an LLM to generate tokens at points of reasoning divergence, but these external calls introduce substantial latency and costs. Alternatively, standard distillation is often hindered by the capacity limitation, as SLMs struggle to accurately mimic the LLM's complex generative distribution. We address this dilemma by identifying local sufficiency: at divergence points, the LLM's preferred token consistently resides within the SLM's top-K next-token predictions, even when failing to emerge as the SLM top-1 choice. We therefore propose SELECT TO THINK (S2T), which reframes the LLM's role from open-ended generation to selection among the SLM's proposals, simplifying the supervision signal to discrete candidate rankings. Leveraging this, we introduce S2T-LOCAL, which distills the selection logic into the SLM, empowering it to perform autonomous re-ranking without inference-time LLM dependency. Empirically, we demonstrate that a 1.5B SLM's top-8 candidates capture the 32B LLM's choice with 95% hit rate. Translating this potential into performance, S2T-LOCAL improves greedy decoding by 24.1% on average across benchmarks, effectively matching the efficacy of 8-path self-consistency while operating with single-trajectory efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [Turning the TIDE: Cross-Architecture Distillation for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26951)
*Gongbo Zhang,Wen Wang,Ye Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE是首个跨架构扩散大语言模型蒸馏框架，通过三个模块（TIDAL、CompDemo、Reverse CALM）将8B密集模型和16B MoE模型蒸馏到0.6B学生模型，在8个基准上平均提升1.53分，HumanEval代码生成达48.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型需要数十亿参数才能达到竞争性能，已有的蒸馏方法仅针对同架构内的推理步数缩减，无法解决师生模型在架构、注意力机制和分词器不同的跨架构知识迁移问题。

Method: 提出TIDE框架，包含三个模块：(1) TIDAL——联合调节训练进度和扩散时间步的蒸馏强度，考虑教师模型随噪声变化的可靠性；(2) CompDemo——通过互补掩码分割丰富教师上下文，改善高掩码率下的预测；(3) Reverse CALM——跨分词器目标函数，通过逆分块级似然匹配实现有界梯度和双端噪声过滤。

Result: 将8B密集模型和16B MoE模型蒸馏到0.6B学生模型，在两个异构流水线上平均超越基线1.53分，HumanEval代码生成从32.3提升至48.78。

Conclusion: TIDE是首个跨架构扩散大语言模型蒸馏框架，通过三个创新模块有效解决了师生架构、注意力机制和分词器不同带来的迁移挑战，显著提升了小模型在代码生成等任务上的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer parallel decoding and bidirectional context, but state-of-the-art dLLMs require billions of parameters for competitive performance. While existing distillation methods for dLLMs reduce inference steps within a single architecture, none address cross-architecture knowledge transfer, in which the teacher and student differ in architecture, attention mechanism, and tokenizer. We present TIDE, the first framework for cross-architecture dLLM distillation, comprising three modular components: (1) TIDAL, which jointly modulates distillation strength across training progress and diffusion timestep to account for the teacher's noise-dependent reliability; (2) CompDemo, which enriches the teacher's context via complementary mask splitting to improve predictions under heavy masking; and (3) Reverse CALM, a cross-tokenizer objective that inverts chunk-level likelihood matching, yielding bounded gradients and dual-end noise filtering. Distilling 8B dense and 16B MoE teachers into a 0.6B student via two heterogeneous pipelines outperforms the baseline by an average of 1.53 points across eight benchmarks, yielding notable gains in code generation, where HumanEval scores reach 48.78 compared to 32.3 for the AR baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [ProMax: Exploring the Potential of LLM-derived Profiles with Distribution Shaping for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26231)
*Yi Zhang,Yiwen Zhang,Kai Zheng,Tong Chen,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ProMax，一种基于分布重塑的推荐框架，从检索视角重新审视用户画像，通过双重分布重塑过程将画像分布作为引导信号，使推荐模型学习用户对未观测物品的偏好，在三个公共数据集上显著提升了四种经典推荐方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐方法中，用户画像虽然能增强推荐模型，但其在特征空间中的增强机制尚不清晰，且现有非线性对齐与融合策略导致语义丢失，未能充分发挥画像潜力。

Method: 从检索视角出发，首先利用稠密检索揭示用户画像与物品画像在特征空间中的协同关系，然后提出双重分布重塑过程，将画像分布作为引导信号，使推荐模型学习用户对未观测物品的偏好。

Result: 在三个公共数据集上对四种经典推荐方法进行实验，ProMax显著提升了基模型性能，并超越了现有基于LLM的推荐方法。

Conclusion: ProMax通过分布重塑的方式有效利用用户画像，避免了语义丢失问题，为基于LLM的通用推荐提供了一种简单而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The remarkable text understanding and generation capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have revitalized the field of general recommendation based on implicit user feedback. Rather than deploying LLMs directly as recommendation models, a more flexible paradigm leverages their ability to interpret users' historical interactions and semantic contexts to extract structured profiles that characterize user preferences. These profiles can be further transformed into actionable high-dimensional representations, serving as powerful signals to augment and strengthen recommendation models. However, the mechanism by which such profiles enhance recommendation performance within the feature space remains insufficiently understood. Moreover, existing studies predominantly rely on nonlinear alignment and fusion strategies to incorporate these profiles, which often lead to semantic loss and fail to fully exploit their potential. To address these limitations, we revisit profiles from a retrieval perspective and propose a simple yet effective recommendation framework built upon distribution shaping (ProMax) in this paper. We begin by employing dense retrieval to uncover the collaborative relationships between user and item profiles within the feature space. Based on this insight, we introduce a dual distribution-reshaping process, in which the profile distribution acts as a guiding signal to steer the recommendation model toward learning user preferences for unseen items beyond the scope of observed interactions. We apply ProMax to four classic recommendation methods on three public datasets. The results indicate that ProMax substantially improves base model performance and outperforms existing LLM-based recommendation approaches.

</details>


### [122] [TimeMM: Time-as-Operator Spectral Filtering for Dynamic Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26247)
*Wei Yang,Rui Zhong,Zihan Lin,Xiaodan Wang,Cheng Chen,Huan Ren,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TimeMM提出了一种时间条件谱滤波框架，通过将交互时效性映射为参数化时间核、自适应谱滤波和模态路由机制，有效建模动态多模态推荐中用户兴趣的非平稳演化，在保持线性时间可扩展性的同时显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐系统大多依赖静态交互图或粗粒度时间启发式方法，无法建模用户兴趣随时间的连续演化和非平稳动态变化，尤其在多模态场景下视觉和文本线索在不同时间域的主导作用不同，这一问题被进一步放大。

Method: TimeMM包含三个核心组件：(1) Time-as-Operator：将交互时效性映射为参数化时间核族，无需显式特征分解即可重加权用户-物品图边权重；(2) Adaptive Spectral Filtering：根据时间上下文混合算子库，产生预测特定的有效谱响应；(3) Spectral-Aware Modality Routing：基于相同时间上下文校准视觉和文本模态的贡献；(4) Spectral Diversity Regularization：在排序空间中鼓励互补专家行为，防止滤波器坍塌。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，TimeMM在保持线性时间可扩展性的同时，一致性地优于最先进的多模态推荐方法。

Conclusion: TimeMM通过时间条件谱滤波有效解决了动态多模态推荐中用户兴趣非平稳演化建模问题，验证了时间作为算子这一范式的有效性，为多模态推荐中的时序动态建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation improves user modeling by integrating collaborative signals with heterogeneous item content. In real applications, user interests evolve over time and exhibit nonstationary dynamics, where different preference factors change at different rates. This challenge is amplified in multimodal settings because visual and textual cues can dominate decisions under different temporal regimes. Despite strong progress, most multimodal recommenders still rely on static interaction graphs or coarse temporal heuristics, which limits their ability to model continuous preference evolution with fine-grained temporal adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose TimeMM, a time-conditioned spectral filtering framework for dynamic multimodal recommendation. TimeMM instantiates Time-as-Operator by mapping interaction recency to a family of parametric temporal kernels that reweight edges on the user--item graph, producing component-specific representations without explicit eigendecomposition. To capture non-stationary interests, we introduce Adaptive Spectral Filtering that mixes the operator bank according to temporal context, yielding prediction-specific effective spectral responses. To account for modality-specific temporal sensitivity, we further propose Spectral-Aware Modality Routing that calibrates visual and textual contributions conditioned on the same temporal context. Finally, a ranking-space Spectral Diversity Regularization encourages complementary expert behaviors and prevents filter-bank collapse. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that TimeMM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multimodal recommenders while maintaining linear-time scalability.

</details>


### [123] [Explaining the "Why": A Unified Framework for the Additive Attribution of Changes in Arbitrary Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26266)
*Changsheng Zhou,Dajun Chen,Zhitao Shen,wei jiang,Yong Li,Peng Di*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于合作博弈论的归因分析框架，通过对度量进行分类，实现从通用近似到精确闭式解的算法谱系，在泛化性、全面性和可解释性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法缺乏一个同时满足三个目标（通用性：适用于任意度量；全面性：同时考虑数据维度和度量组合；严谨性：可解释性强）的统一解决方案。

Method: 基于合作博弈论重新定义归因问题，核心创新是对度量进行数学结构分类，从而设计出从通用近似算法到精确闭式解的完整算法谱系，在泛化性和性能之间实现原则性权衡。

Result: 模拟实验验证了数值准确性；对非可加性度量的实验证明了泛化性；辛普森悖论案例展示了独特的可解释性；最终实验证明其在实际根因分析系统中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在通用性、全面性和可解释性三个维度上均超越现有归因方法，为聚合度量变化解释提供了统一且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Explaining why aggregated measures change is a critical challenge in data analytics that existing systems struggle to address. While current attribution methods exist, they lack a unified solution that is simultaneously general for arbitrary measures, holistic across both data dimensions and measure composition, and rigorous in its interpretability. To bridge this gap, we introduce a principled framework that reframes attribution through the powerful lens of cooperative game theory. Our key contribution is a classification of measures based on their mathematical structure, which enables a spectrum of algorithms-from general approximations to exact, closed-form solutions-that offer a principled trade-off between generality and performance. We demonstrate our framework's superiority through a multi-faceted evaluation: simulations first confirm its numerical accuracy and then its generality for non-additive measures; a case study on Simpson's Paradox showcases its unique interpretability; and a final experiment proves its practical utility by significantly outperforming existing root cause analysis systems.

</details>


### [124] [Meta-Learning and Targeted Differential Privacy to Improve the Accuracy-Privacy Trade-off in Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26390)
*Peter Müllner,Dominik Kowald,Markus Schedl,Elisabeth Lex*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合目标差分隐私（Targeted DP）和元学习（Meta-learning）的方法，在数据层仅对最可能暴露敏感属性的用户数据施加DP扰动，在模型层利用元学习增强对剩余噪声的鲁棒性，从而在推荐精度和用户隐私之间实现更优的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）引入的噪声会降低推荐系统的准确性，如何在保护隐私的同时保持推荐精度是一个关键挑战。

Method: 数据层面：仅对最刻板的、可能泄露敏感属性（如性别、年龄）的用户数据施加DP扰动（Targeted DP）。模型层面：利用元学习（Meta-learning）提高模型对残余DP噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 相比均匀施加DP和全DP基线方法，该方法在精度和隐私之间取得了更优的权衡：元学习提升了精度，Targeted DP降低了经验隐私风险。

Conclusion: 在数据层选择性施加DP，同时在模型层结合元学习，能够有效平衡推荐精度与用户隐私。

Abstract: Balancing differential privacy (DP) with recommendation accuracy is a key challenge in privacy-preserving recommender systems, since DP-noise degrades accuracy. We address this trade-off at both the data and model levels. At the data level, we apply DP only to the most stereotypical user data likely to reveal sensitive attributes, such as gender or age, to reduce unnecessary perturbation; we refer to this as targeted DP. At the model level, we use meta-learning to improve robustness to remaining DP-noise. This achieves a better trade-off between accuracy and privacy than standard approaches: Meta-learning improves accuracy and targeted DP leads to lower empirical privacy risk compared to uniformly applied DP and full DP baselines. Overall, our findings show that selectively applying DP at the data level together with meta-learning at the model level can effectively balance recommendation accuracy and user privacy.

</details>


### [125] [CARD: Non-Uniform Quantization of Visual Semantic Unit for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26427)
*Yibiao Wei,Jie Zou,Pengfei Zhang,Xiao Ao,Weikang Guo,Zeyu Ma,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式推荐框架CARD，通过视觉语义单元统一多模态信号进行整体语义建模，并设计非均匀量化框架NU-RQ-VAE解决语义嵌入分布不均问题，在多个数据集上取得优于基线方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐框架中的语义ID学习面临两个关键挑战：(1) 两阶段范式（SID构建与自回归生成）对异构融合的监督不足，难以学习高质量SID；(2) 非均匀嵌入导致码字不平衡和生成偏差。

Method: CARD框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 视觉语义单元（Visual Semantic Unit），在编码前将文本、视觉和协同信号统一到结构化视觉表示中，实现整体语义建模；(2) 非均匀量化框架NU-RQ-VAE，通过可学习且可逆的非均匀变换将偏斜的语义分布映射到更均衡的潜在空间，提升码本利用率和量化精度。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，CARD在各种设置下均优于基线方法；非均匀变换模块是即插即用的，在不同量化方案下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CARD通过统一多模态语义建模和解决嵌入分布不均问题，有效提升了生成式推荐中语义ID的质量，为生成式推荐的发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative recommendation frameworks typically represent items as discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs). While existing studies have sought to enhance SID construction by incorporating multimodal content, collaborative signals, or more advanced quantization techniques, learning high-quality SIDs still faces two key challenges: (1) The two-stage generative recommendation paradigm (SID construction and autoregressive generation) provides insufficient supervision for heterogeneous fusion, which hinders learning high-quality SIDs, and (2) non-uniform embeddings lead to codeword imbalance and generation bias. To address these challenges, we propose a novel generative recommendation framework, called CARD. CARD introduces a visual semantic unit that unifies textual, visual, and collaborative signals into a structured visual representation prior to encoding, enabling holistic semantic modeling and effectively alleviating the semantic gap, thereby reducing the reliance on supervision signals during SID learning. Furthermore, to deal with the highly non-uniform distribution of item semantic embeddings in recommendation scenarios, we develop a non-uniform quantization framework (NU-RQ-VAE), which incorporates a learnable and invertible non-uniform transformation into the quantization process to map skewed semantic distributions into a more balanced latent space, thereby significantly improving codebook utilization and quantization accuracy. Experiments on multiple datasets show that CARD consistently outperforms baseline methods under various settings; meanwhile, the proposed non-uniform transformation module is plug-and-play and remains robust across different quantization schemes. Code is available at https://github.com/HAI-UESTC/CARD.

</details>


### [126] [Efficient Listwise Reranking with Compressed Document Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26483)
*Hervé Déjean,Stéphane Clinchant*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RRK利用文档压缩技术，将文档压缩为多词元的固定大小嵌入表示，通过蒸馏训练实现高效列表式重排序。8B参数的模型运行速度比0.6-4B参数的小型重排序器快3-18倍，同时效果相当或更优，尤其在长文档基准上优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重排序在使用大语言模型时计算成本高昂，现有缓解方法（使用小模型或控制输入长度）仍有局限。受RAG中文档压缩技术的启发，探索是否能通过文档压缩表示结合列表式重排序来兼顾效率与效果。

Method: 提出RRK列表式重排序器，将文档压缩为多词元固定大小的嵌入表示；通过蒸馏（distillation）方式进行简单训练；结合压缩表示与列表式重排序实现高效系统。

Result: 8B参数模型运行速度比0.6-4B参数的小型重排序器快3-18倍，同时效果匹配或更优；在长文档基准上效率优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 压缩表示与列表式重排序的组合可构建高效且有效的重排序系统，RRK在效率和效果上均优于更小的重排序器，尤其适用于长文档场景。

Abstract: Reranking, the process of refining the output from a first-stage retriever, is often considered computationally expensive, especially when using Large Language Models (LLMs). A common approach to mitigate this cost involves utilizing smaller LLMs or controlling input length. Inspired by recent advances in document compression for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), we introduce RRK, an efficient and effective listwise reranker compressing documents into multi-token fixed-size embedding representations. Our simple training via distillation shows that this combination of rich compressed representations and listwise reranking yields a highly efficient and effective system. In particular, our 8B-parameter model runs 3x-18x faster than smaller rerankers (0.6-4B parameters) while matching or outperforming them in effectiveness. The efficiency gains are even more striking on long-document benchmarks, where RRK widens its advantage further.

</details>


### [127] [When to Retrieve During Reasoning: Adaptive Retrieval for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26649)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReaLM-Retrieve 是一个面向大型推理模型的推理感知型检索框架，通过步骤级不确定性检测、检索干预策略和效率优化机制，解决了推理模型与RAG之间的根本性不匹配问题。在MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA上，该方法平均F1提升10.1%，检索调用减少47%，在MuSiQue上仅需1.8次检索即达71.2% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1、OpenAI o1）生成的长链推理需要多步过程中的证据注入，而传统RAG系统仅在推理前提供上下文，两者存在根本性的对齐问题。

Method: 提出三项核心创新：(1) 步骤级不确定性检测器，在推理步骤粒度识别知识缺口；(2) 学习最优时机注入外部证据的检索干预策略；(3) 将每次检索开销降低3.2倍的效率优化集成机制。

Result: 在MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultiHopQA上平均F1绝对提升10.1%（9.0-11.8%），检索调用比IRCoT减少47%（p<0.01）。MuSiQue上71.2% F1仅需1.8次检索/问题。检索质量本身也提升，Recall@5达81.3%，精度和MRR均优于固定间隔基线。

Conclusion: ReaLM-Retrieve 为推理密集型检索任务建立了新的效率-准确率权衡最优标准，有效弥合了推理模型与RAG之间的对齐差距。

Abstract: Large reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1 and OpenAI o1 generate extended chains of thought spanning thousands of tokens, yet their integration with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) remains fundamentally misaligned. Current RAG systems optimize for providing context before reasoning begins, while reasoning models require evidence injection during multi-step inference chains. We introduce ReaLM-Retrieve, a reasoning-aware retrieval framework that addresses this mismatch through three key innovations: (1) a step-level uncertainty detector that identifies knowledge gaps at reasoning-step granularity rather than token or sentence level; (2) a retrieval intervention policy that learns when external evidence maximally benefits ongoing reasoning; and (3) an efficiency-optimized integration mechanism that reduces per-retrieval overhead by 3.2x compared to naive integration. Experiments on MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ReaLM-Retrieve achieves on average 10.1% absolute improvement in answer F1 over standard RAG (range: 9.0-11.8% across the three benchmarks) while reducing retrieval calls by 47% compared to fixed-interval approaches like IRCoT (all improvements significant at p<0.01, paired bootstrap). On the challenging MuSiQue benchmark requiring 2-4 hop reasoning, our method achieves 71.2% F1 with an average of only 1.8 retrieval calls per question. Analysis shows that ReaLM-Retrieve also improves retrieval quality itself, achieving 81.3% Recall@5 with consistently higher precision and MRR than fixed-interval baselines on supporting evidence, establishing new state-of-the-art efficiency-accuracy trade-offs for reasoning-intensive retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [128] [The Bandit's Blind Spot: The Critical Role of User State Representation in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26651)
*Pedro R. Pires,Gregorio F. Azevedo,Rafael T. Sereicikas,Pietro L. Campos,Tiago A. Almeida*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵分解模型生成的嵌入表示作为用户状态表征对上下文多臂老虎机推荐算法性能的影响，发现状态表征的变化带来的性能提升可能超过更换算法本身，且没有一种嵌入或聚合策略在所有数据集上占优，强调了需要整体考虑嵌入质量和状态构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中用户状态表征（即用户交互历史的嵌入表示）对CMAB算法性能的影响尚未被充分探索，现有研究大多关注算法创新而忽视了状态表征这一关键组件。

Method: 通过大规模实验，比较不同矩阵分解模型生成的嵌入表示及聚合策略作为用户状态对传统CMAB算法性能的影响。

Result: 状态表征的变化带来的性能提升大于更换bandit算法本身；没有单一的嵌入或聚合策略在所有数据集上占优。

Conclusion: 推进基于bandit的推荐系统需要整体方法，在算法创新的同时优先考虑嵌入质量和状态构建。

Abstract: With the increasing availability of online information, recommender systems have become an important tool for many web-based systems. Due to the continuous aspect of recommendation environments, these systems increasingly rely on contextual multi-armed bandits (CMAB) to deliver personalized and real-time suggestions. A critical yet underexplored component in these systems is the representation of user state, which typically encapsulates the user's interaction history and is deeply correlated with the model's decisions and learning. In this paper, we investigate the impact of different embedding-based state representations derived from matrix factorization models on the performance of traditional CMAB algorithms. Our large-scale experiments reveal that variations in state representation can lead to improvements greater than those achieved by changing the bandit algorithm itself. Furthermore, no single embedding or aggregation strategy consistently dominates across datasets, underscoring the need for domain-specific evaluation. These results expose a substantial gap in the literature and emphasize that advancing bandit-based recommender systems requires a holistic approach that prioritizes embedding quality and state construction alongside algorithmic innovation. The source code for our experiments is publicly available on https://github.com/UFSCar-LaSID/bandits_blind_spot.

</details>


### [129] [Factorized Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26760)
*Tianqi Gao,Chengkai Huang,Zihan Wang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出因子化潜在推理（FLR）框架，将LLM推荐中的用户偏好推理分解为多个解耦的偏好因子，通过轻量级多头注意力模块、正交性和多样性正则化以及强化学习对齐，提升顺序推荐的性能、鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐方法通常用单一潜在向量表示用户意图，难以捕捉用户偏好多面性的本质。作者旨在通过分解潜在推理为多个解耦因子来解决这一局限。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级多因子注意力模块，迭代优化潜在思想表征，每个因子关注用户交互历史的不同方面；2) 引入正交性、注意力多样性和稀疏性正则化目标促进因子多样化和专精化；3) 动态聚合各因子贡献进行最终预测；4) 集成基于组相对策略优化的强化学习策略，在潜在推理空间中实现稳定对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，FLR一致优于强基线方法，同时提升了鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 因子化潜在推理能有效捕捉用户偏好的多面性，结合多样性正则化和强化学习对齐后，可显著提升LLM顺序推荐的效果与可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adopted for recommendation by framing user preference modeling as a language generation problem. However, existing latent reasoning approaches typically represent user intent with a single latent vector, which struggles to capture the inherently multi-faceted nature of user preferences. We propose Factorized Latent Reasoning (FLR), a novel framework for LLM-based sequential recommendation that decomposes latent reasoning into multiple disentangled preference factors. FLR introduces a lightweight multi-factor attention module that iteratively refines a latent thought representation, where each factor attends to distinct aspects of the user's interaction history. To encourage diversity and specialization, we design orthogonality, attention diversity, and sparsity regularization objectives, and dynamically aggregate factor contributions for the final prediction. We further integrate FLR with an efficient reinforcement learning strategy based on group-relative policy optimization, enabling stable alignment directly in the latent reasoning space. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that FLR consistently outperforms strong baselines while improving robustness and interpretability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [A Multimodal and Explainable Machine Learning Approach to Diagnosing Multi-Class Ejection Fraction from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25942)
*Catherine Ning,Yu Ma,Cindy Beini Wang,Sean McMahon,Joseph Radojevic,Steven Zweibel,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合12导联心电图时序特征与结构化EHR数据的多模态机器学习框架，用于将左心室射血分数（LVEF）分为四个临床常用分层，在366,784对心电图-超声心动图数据上训练XGBoost模型，实现了较优的分类性能，并在时间泛化验证中保持稳定，为资源有限环境下的LVEF筛查提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LVEF评估依赖超声心动图，在初级保健和资源匮乏地区可及性有限，亟需一种更便捷、低成本的筛查方法。

Method: 构建多模态机器学习框架，融合12导联心电图的工程化时序特征与结构化EHR变量，使用XGBoost模型将LVEF分为正常（>50%）、轻度降低（40-50%）、中度降低（30-40%）、重度降低（<30%）四类，并利用SHAP进行特征重要性解释。基于Hartford HealthCare回顾性数据，训练集包含30,952名门诊患者的36,784对ECG-超声心动图数据，时间泛化验证集为后续时期的19,966份ECG。

Result: 多模态模型在四分类任务中取得了一VS其余AUROC：重度降低0.95、中度降低0.92、轻度降低0.82、正常0.91，优于仅使用ECG或仅使用EHR的基线模型，且在时间验证集上保持性能稳定。

Conclusion: 基于心电图的多模态LVEF分层方法可作为实用的筛查与分诊工具，在资源有限的场景下优先安排确认性影像学检查。

Abstract: Left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF) assessment depends on echocardiography, limiting access in primary care and resource-constrained settings. We developed a multimodal machine-learning framework that combines engineered 12-lead ECG timeseries features with structured EHR variables to classify LVEF into four clinically used strata: normal (>50%), mildly reduced (40-50%), moderately reduced (30-40%), and severely reduced (<30%). To support model explainability, we identified the most influential ECG and EHR features via SHAP attributions. Using retrospective data from Hartford HealthCare, we trained XGBoost models on 36,784 ECG-echocardiogram pairs from 30,952 outpatients and evaluated temporal generalizability on 19,966 ECGs from a subsequent period. The multimodal model achieved one-vs-rest AUROCs of 0.95 (severe), 0.92 (moderate), 0.82 (mild), and 0.91 (normal), outperforming ECG-only and EHR-only baselines, and maintained performance under temporal validation. This work supports ECG-based, multimodal LVEF stratification as a practical screening and triage aid to prioritize confirmatory imaging where resources are limited.

</details>


### [131] [Open Problems in Frontier AI Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25982)
*Marta Ziosi,Miro Plueckebaum,Stephen Casper,Henry Papadatos,Ze Shen Chin,Peter Slattery,James Gealy,Tim G. J. Rudner,Brian Tse,Ariel Gil,Patricia Paskov,Maximilian Negele,Rokas Gipiškis,Nada Madkour,Vera Lummis,Rupal Jain,Luise Eder,Kristina Fort,Malou C. van Draanen Glismann,Inès Belhadj,Amin Oueslati,Anna K. Wisakanto,Richard Mallah,Koen Holtman,Ranj Zuhdi,Daniel S. Schiff,Jessica Newman,Malcolm Murray,Robert Trager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地梳理了前沿人工智能风险管理中的开放性问题，将问题分类为科学/技术共识缺乏、与既有风险管理框架的错位、以及执行层面的不足，并识别了最适宜解决每类问题的行动者，旨在为未来的研究和治理提供问题导向的议程参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI既放大了现有风险，又带来了全新的挑战。技术变革速度过快导致缺乏稳定的科学共识，同时新兴的前沿AI安全实践往往与既有的风险管理框架不匹配甚至相互削弱。因此需要系统地梳理这些开放性问题，以推动形成稳健且有意义的共识。

Method: 采用问题导向的方法，通过结构化文献综述，依次审视风险管理流程的每个阶段（风险规划、识别、分析、评估、缓解），识别未解决的挑战及最适宜解决它们的行动者。将开放性问题分为三类：（a）缺乏科学或技术共识；（b）与既有风险管理框架错位或对其构成挑战；（c）虽有共识和一致但执行存在不足。

Result: 产出了一份问题导向的议程设定参考文件，并辅以在线动态知识库，映射了前沿AI风险管理中的开放性问题及其对应的行动者（开发者、部署者、监管者、标准制定机构、研究人员、第三方评估者），旨在促进协调、减少重复工作、指导未来研究和治理努力。

Conclusion: 本文不提供具体解决方案，而是通过系统性地揭示问题空间和责任主体，为前沿AI风险管理的科学共识形成、框架协调和执行改进指明方向，推动该领域的协调行动和有效治理。

Abstract: Frontier AI both amplifies existing risks and introduces qualitatively novel challenges. Not only is there a notable lack of stable scientific consensus resulting from the rapid pace of technological change, but emerging frontier AI safety practices are often misaligned with, or may undermine, established risk management frameworks. To address these challenges, we systematically surface open problems in frontier AI risk management. Adopting a problem-oriented approach, we examine each stage of the risk management process - risk planning, identification, analysis, evaluation, and mitigation - through a structured review of the literature, identifying unresolved challenges and the actors best positioned to address them. Recognising that different types of open problems call for different responses, we classify open problems according to whether they reflect (a) a lack of scientific or technical consensus, (b) misalignment with, or challenges to, established risk management frameworks, or (c) shortcomings in implementation despite apparent consensus and alignment. By mapping these open problems and identifying the actors best positioned to address them - including developers, deployers, regulators, standards bodies, researchers, and third-party evaluators - this work aims to clarify where progress is needed to enable robust and meaningful consensus on frontier AI risk management.The paper does not propose specific solutions; instead, it provides a problem-oriented, agenda-setting reference document, complemented by a living online repository, intended to support coordination, reduce duplication, and guide future research and governance efforts.

</details>


### [132] [Correcting Performance Estimation Bias in Imbalanced Classification with Minority Subconcepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26024)
*Taylor Maxson,Roberto Corizzo,Yaning Wu,Nathalie Japkowicz,Colin Bellinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测加权平衡准确率（pBA）方法，利用多类子概念模型的后验概率作为期望效用权重，在子概念标签不可用时对类别内子概念分布不均导致的评估偏差进行修正，实验表明pBA比未加权指标更稳定和可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级评估会掩盖同一类内不同子概念之间的性能差异，导致平均表现良好的模型在特定子群体上失败。传统的不平衡分类评估指标偏向于较大的少数子概念，而基于真实子概念标签的效用加权虽可缓解此偏差，但测试时这些标签通常不可用。

Method: 提出预测加权平衡准确率（pBA），用多类别子概念模型预测的后验概率替代不可用的子概念真实标签，将评估权重定义为该后验下的期望效用，从而得到一种软性的、具有不确定性意识的评估指标。

Result: 在表格基准数据集、医学影像和文本数据集上的实验表明，在类别内异质性存在时，未加权得分可能具有误导性，而pBA在子概念分布不均匀但非病态的情况下能提供更稳定和可解释的评估结果。

Conclusion: pBA作为一种实用的效用加权评估方法，能在子概念标签缺失时有效纠正类别内不平衡导致的评估偏差，为模型在子群体层面提供更可靠的性能度量。

Abstract: Class-level evaluation can conceal substantial performance disparities across subconcepts within the same class, causing models that perform well on average to fail on specific subpopulations. Prior work has shown that common evaluation measures for imbalanced classification are biased toward larger minority subconcepts and that utility-based reweighting using true subconcept labels can mitigate this bias; however, such labels are rarely available at test time. We introduce a practical utility-weighted evaluation that replaces unavailable subconcept labels with predicted posterior probabilities from a multiclass subconcept model. Evaluation weights are defined as the expected utility under this posterior, yielding a soft, uncertainty-aware metric we call predicted-weighted balanced accuracy (pBA). Experiments on tabular benchmarks as well as medical-imaging and text datasets show that unweighted scores can be misleading under within-class heterogeneity, while pBA provides more stable and interpretable assessments when subconcept distributions are uneven but not pathological. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/correcting-bias-imbalance-9C6C/.

</details>


### [133] [RaMP: Runtime-Aware Megakernel Polymorphism for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26039)
*Vyom Sharma,Debajyoti Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RaMP是一个面向MoE推理的路由感知调度框架，通过性能区域分析和四参数波代价模型，根据运行时专家路由分布动态选择最优kernel配置，在不同架构上实现1.13x-1.41x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE推理系统仅根据batch size进行调度，忽略了专家路由分布对kernel吞吐量的影响，导致10-70%的吞吐量未被充分利用。

Method: 1) 性能区域分析：仅从硬件常数推导出每种优化适用的条件，正确预测8种架构（含3种未见过的）；2) 四参数波代价模型：根据运行时专家直方图选择最快配置，仅需每模型10-24分钟一次性profiling，平均遗憾率0.93%；3) 与CuTe DSL kernel配合，暴露134-268种多态配置。

Result: vs 静态调度: 1.22x kernel加速; vLLM端到端: vs Triton 1.30x, vs DeepGEMM 1.41x, vs FlashInfer CUTLASS 1.13x; 应用于Alpha-MoE无需修改源码即获1.14x加速。

Conclusion: 路由感知调度是释放MoE推理潜力的关键，代价模型仅依赖CTA网格几何结构，具有kernel无关性，可广泛适用。

Abstract: The optimal kernel configuration for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference depends on both batch size and the expert routing distribution, yet production systems dispatch from batch size alone, leaving 10-70% of kernel throughput unrealized. We present RaMP, a routing-aware dispatch framework. A performance-region analysis derives, from hardware constants alone, when each optimization helps, correctly predicting all 8 tested architectures, including 3 unseen. A four-parameter wave cost model selects the fastest configuration from the runtime expert histogram, achieving 0.93% mean regret versus exhaustive search, fitted from just 10-24 minutes of one-time profiling per model. Because the model depends only on CTA grid geometry, it is kernel-agnostic: applied to Alpha-MoE, it delivers 1.14x with no source modification. Paired with a co-designed CuTe DSL kernel exposing 134-268 polymorphic configurations, RaMP delivers 1.22x kernel speedup over static dispatch and 1.30x end-to-end speedup in vLLM serving over Triton, 1.41x over DeepGEMM, and 1.13x over FlashInfer CUTLASS.

</details>


### [134] [Observable Neural ODEs for Identifiable Causal Forecasting in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26070)
*Jennifer Wendland,Nicolas Freitag,Maik Kschischo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可观测神经ODE（ObsNODEs），将控制理论中的可观测性与因果可识别性联系起来，在具有隐藏混杂因素的连续时间序列决策问题中实现动态治疗效应的因果推断，并在多个数据集上表现优于现有序列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间序列决策问题中存在隐藏混杂因素，这些混杂因素同时影响治疗和结果，导致动态治疗效应的因果推断困难。现有方法未能充分利用控制理论中的可观测性概念来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 理论层面：证明在潜状态空间模型中，潜动力学的可观测性（来自控制理论）是实现动态治疗效应因果可识别性的必要条件，并推导出连续时间调整公式。2) 模型层面：提出ObsNODEs，即采用可观测标准形式的神经ODE模型，确保潜状态可从观测数据中重构，从而支持反事实结果预测。

Result: 在合成癌症数据、基于MIMIC-IV的半合成数据以及真实脓毒症数据上，ObsNODEs在反事实预测任务中显著优于最新的序列模型。

Conclusion: 控制理论中的可观测性与因果可识别性之间存在理论联系，基于可观测标准形态的神经ODE模型能够有效解决连续时间动态治疗效应中的隐藏混杂问题，在医疗等领域的因果预测任务中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Causal inference in continuous-time sequential decision problems is challenged by hidden confounders. We show that, in latent state-space models with time-varying interventions, observability of the latent dynamics from observed data is necessary for identifying dynamic treatment effects, linking control-theoretic observability to causal identifiability, even when hidden confounders affect both treatments and outcomes. We derive a continuous-time adjustment formula expressing potential outcome distributions under treatment trajectories via the measurement model, latent dynamics, and the filtering distribution over latent states given observed histories. We propose Observable Neural ODEs (ObsNODEs), Neural ODE models in observable normal form for causal forecasting. ObsNODEs learn continuous-time dynamics with states reconstructible from observations, enabling outcome prediction under alternative treatment paths. Experiments on synthetic cancer data, semi-synthetic data based on MIMIC-IV, and real-world sepsis data show strong performance over recent sequence models.

</details>


### [135] [PPG-Based Affect Recognition with Long-Range Deep Models: A Measurement-Driven Comparison of CNN, Transformer, and Mamba Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26078)
*Karim Alghoul,Hussein Al Osman,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了CNN、CNN-LSTM混合、Transformer和Mamba四种深度学习架构在基于PPG信号的情感识别（唤醒度、效价、放松状态）中的表现。结果表明，虽然Transformer和Mamba性能与CNN基线相当，但CNN在准确率和模型大小方面整体最优，Transformer在唤醒度和放松状态的F1分数上略有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来Transformer和Mamba等长序列模型在自然语言和时间序列任务中表现优异，但它们在PPG情感识别这类小样本、高噪声数据集上是否优于传统的CNN和LSTM尚不明确，缺乏系统的对比研究。

Method: 在统一的数据预处理、分割和训练流程下，采用受试者独立的5折交叉验证，对CNN、CNN-LSTM混合、Transformer和Mamba四种架构在基于腕部PPG信号的唤醒度、效价和放松状态分类任务上进行性能比较。

Result: Transformer和Mamba的性能与CNN基线相当，但并非在所有任务上一致优于CNN。CNN在准确率和模型大小方面整体最优，Transformer在唤醒度和放松状态的F1分数上表现更好。

Conclusion: 对于PPG情感识别任务，CNN仍然是综合效果最好的选择；该研究首次评估了Transformer和Mamba在PPG情感识别中的应用，为可穿戴情感监测系统的模型选择提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG) is increasingly used in wearable affective computing due to its low cost and ease of integration into consumer devices. Recent advances in deep learning have introduced long-range sequence models, such as Transformers, and state-space models, like Mamba, which have demonstrated strong performance on natural language and general time-series tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these architectures offer tangible benefits over widely used Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTMs) for PPG-based affect recognition, given that datasets are typically small and noisy. This work presents a measurement-driven comparison of four deep learning architectures, CNN, CNN-LSTM hybrid, Transformers, and Mamba, for classifying arousal, valence, and relaxation states from wrist-based PPG signals. All models are evaluated under a subject-independent 5-fold cross-validation protocol using identical preprocessing, segmentation, and training pipelines. Our results show that the Transformer and Mamba models achieve performance comparable to that of a CNN baseline, but do not consistently outperform it across all tasks. CNNs remain the most effective overall, providing the highest accuracy with the smallest model size, whereas Transformers have a better balance of F1 scores for Arousal and Relaxation. The study provides the first evaluation of Transformer and Mamba models for PPG-based affect recognition, offering practical guidance on model selection for wearable affective monitoring systems.

</details>


### [136] [reward-lens: A Mechanistic Interpretability Library for Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26130)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出reward-lens开源库，将机械可解释性工具移植到奖励模型，核心发现是线性归因无法预测因果补丁效应（斯皮尔曼相关系数-0.256和-0.027）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性工具（logit透镜、直接logit归因、激活补丁、稀疏自编码器）都是为具有词汇表unembedding的生成式LLM设计的，而奖励模型用标量回归头替换了词汇表unembedding，导致这些工具无法直接使用。

Method: 基于"奖励头权重向量w_r是自然解释轴"这一观察，构建了包含奖励透镜、组件归因、三模式激活补丁、奖励破解探针套件、TopK SAE特征归因、跨模型比较等工具的完整可解释性框架，并设计了十方法适配器协议覆盖主流模型架构。

Result: 在Skywork和ArmoRM两个生产级奖励模型上，线性归因与因果补丁效应之间呈负相关（平均斯皮尔曼ρ分别为-0.256和-0.027），表明线性方法不能预测因果效应。

Conclusion: 线性归因与因果补丁之间的差异不是需要修复的bug，而是需要暴露的属性；框架将观察视角和因果视角作为一等公民并使之可直接比较。

Abstract: Every RLHF-trained language model is shaped by a reward model, yet the mechanistic interpretability toolkit -- logit lens, direct logit attribution, activation patching, sparse autoencoders -- was built for generative LLMs whose primitives all project onto a vocabulary unembedding. Reward models replace that with a scalar regression head, breaking each tool. We present reward-lens, an open-source library that ports this toolkit to reward models, organised around one observation: the reward head's weight vector $w_r$ is the natural axis for every interpretability question. The library provides a Reward Lens, component attribution, three-mode activation patching, a reward-hacking probe suite, TopK SAE feature attribution, cross-model comparison, and five theory-grounded extensions (distortion index, divergence-aware patching, misalignment cascade detection, reward-term conflict analysis, concept-vector analysis). A ten-method adapter protocol covers Llama, Mistral, Gemma-2, and ArmoRM multi-objective heads, with a generic adapter for any HuggingFace sequence classification model. We validate on two production reward models across ~695 RewardBench pairs. The central empirical finding is negative: linear attribution does not predict causal patching effects (mean Spearman $ρ= -0.256$ on Skywork, $-0.027$ on ArmoRM). The framework treats this disagreement as a property to expose, not a bug -- motivating a design that keeps observational and causal views first-class and directly comparable.

</details>


### [137] [Understanding DNNs in Feature Interaction Models: A Dimensional Collapse Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26489)
*Jiancheng Wang,Mingjia Yin,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出新视角解释DNN在特征交互推荐模型中的作用：DNN能够缓解嵌入表示的维度坍塌问题，从而提升模型鲁棒性，而非传统认为的隐式捕捉高阶特征交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内长期存在关于DNN在特征交互推荐模型中作用的争议：一方面有人认为DNN能隐式捕获高阶特征交互，另一方面近期研究指出DNN在学习点积（二阶交互）甚至更高阶交互方面存在局限。本文旨在解决这一争议，提出理解DNN有效性的新视角。

Method: 通过在并行DNN和堆叠DNN两种结构上进行大规模实验，对两个特征交互模型中的完整DNN进行整体研究，并对DNN内部组件进行细粒度消融分析。同时结合基于梯度的理论分析与实证验证，揭示维度坍塌的底层机制。

Result: 实验结果表明，并行DNN和堆叠DNN均能有效缓解嵌入表示的维度坍塌问题。基于梯度的理论分析结合实证证据，揭示了维度坍塌的发生机制。

Conclusion: DNN在推荐模型中的有效性主要源于其对表示维度鲁棒性的提升（缓解维度坍塌），而非隐式学习高阶特征交互。这为理解DNN在推荐系统中的作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: DNNs have gained widespread adoption in feature interaction recommendation models. However, there has been a longstanding debate on their roles. On one hand, some works claim that DNNs possess the ability to implicitly capture high-order feature interactions. Conversely, recent studies have highlighted the limitations of DNNs in effectively learning dot products, specifically second-order interactions, let alone higher-order interactions. In this paper, we present a novel perspective to understand the effectiveness of DNNs: their impact on the dimensional robustness of the representations. In particular, we conduct extensive experiments involving both parallel DNNs and stacked DNNs. Our evaluation encompasses an overall study of complete DNN on two feature interaction models, alongside a fine-grained ablation analysis of components within DNNs. Experimental results demonstrate that both parallel and stacked DNNs can effectively mitigate the dimensional collapse of embeddings. Furthermore, a gradient-based theoretical analysis, supported by empirical evidence, uncovers the underlying mechanisms of dimensional collapse.

</details>


### [138] [Spatially-constrained clustering of geospatial features for heat vulnerability assessment of favelas in Rio de Janeiro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26133)
*Baptiste Clemence,Thomas Hallopeau,Vanderlei Pascoal De Matos,Laurent Demagistri,Joris Guerin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过结合空间约束聚类与地表温度分析，开发了一个数据驱动框架来评估里约热内卢贫民窟的热脆弱性，识别出两种贫民窟类型，并发现平坦地形的贫民窟在极端高温事件中比坡地上的贫民窟高出2-3°C，表明聚落形态对热脆弱性有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正规聚落面临着不成比例的气候相关健康危害，但现有方法缺乏系统性途径来将不同的聚落特征与环境健康结果联系起来。

Method: 采用数据驱动框架，结合空间约束聚类与地表温度（LST）分析，利用遥感和地理空间特征对里约热内卢的贫民窟进行分析。

Result: 识别出两种贫民窟类型：平坦地形上新建、交通便利的聚落（Cluster 0）和植被覆盖坡地上历史久远、交通不便的社区（Cluster 1）。在16次极端高温事件中，两类聚落之间的系统温差为2-3°C，平坦地形的贫民窟热暴露显著更高。

Conclusion: 聚落形态关键性地影响热脆弱性，该框架具有可复制性，可为全球非正规聚落的针对性城市规划和公共卫生干预提供支持。

Abstract: Informal settlements face disproportionate exposure to climate-related health hazards. However, existing methodologies lack systematic approaches to link diverse settlement characteristics with environmental health outcomes. We develop a data-driven framework to assess heat vulnerability in Rio de Janeiro's favelas by combining spatially-constrained clustering with land surface temperature (LST) analysis. Using remote sensing and geospatial features, we identify two distinct favela typologies: recent, well-connected settlements on flat terrain (Cluster 0) and historical, poorly-connected communities on vegetated slopes (Cluster 1). Analysis of 16 extreme heat events reveals systematic temperature differences of 2--3$^\circ$C between clusters, with flat-terrain favelas experiencing significantly higher heat exposure. Our findings demonstrate that settlement morphology critically influences heat vulnerability, providing a replicable framework for targeted urban planning and public health interventions in informal settlements globally.

</details>


### [139] [Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits: Bridging Uplift Modeling and Sequential Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26169)
*Abhirami Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算约束因果Bandit（BCCB）框架，将因果推断与在线学习结合，解决冷启动场景下广告预算分配问题。相比传统离线方法需约10,000个历史样本，BCCB从首个用户即可有效运作，且性能方差降低3-5倍，在所有预算水平上优于Thompson Sampling等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字广告中预算约束下的处理分配是关键挑战。传统两阶段离线管线（先估计异质处理效应，再求解约束优化）在数据充足时有效，但在新广告活动、新市场、新客户群体等冷启动场景中因缺乏历史数据而失败。

Method: 提出Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits (BCCB)在线框架，将三个组件统一为单一序贯过程：1）学习个体层面的广告效果；2）探索响应不确定的用户；3）随时间进行预算节奏控制。逐用户进行决策，边学习边支出预算。

Result: 在Criteo Uplift数据集上评估：1）离线方法需约10,000个历史样本才能产生可靠结果，BCCB从首个用户即可有效运作；2）BCCB运行间性能方差低3-5倍；3）在所有预算水平上，BCCB一致优于标准Thompson Sampling、预算化Thompson Sampling和贪婪HTE估计方法。

Conclusion: BCCB框架有效解决了冷启动场景下的广告预算分配问题，具有数据高效性、低方差和稳定优于纯在线方法的性能，更适合实际广告活动规划。

Abstract: Treatment allocation under budget constraints is a central challenge in digital advertising: advertisers must decide which users to show ads to while spending a limited budget wisely. The standard approach follows a two-stage offline pipeline - first collect historical data to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects (HTE), then solve a constrained optimization to allocate the budget. This works well with abundant data, but fails in cold-start settings such as new campaigns, new markets, or new customer segments where little historical data exists. We propose Budget-Constrained Causal Bandits (BCCB), an online framework that learns which users respond to ads while simultaneously spending the budget, making treatment decisions one user at a time. BCCB unifies three components into a single sequential process: learning individual-level ad effectiveness, exploring users whose response is uncertain, and pacing the budget over time. We evaluated on the Criteo Uplift dataset, a large-scale advertising dataset from a real randomized controlled trial. Our key finding is a data-efficiency crossover: offline methods require approximately 10,000 historical observations to produce reliable results, while BCCB operates effectively from the very first user. Furthermore, BCCB exhibits 3-5x lower performance variance between runs, making it more practical for real campaign planning. Among purely online methods, BCCB consistently outperforms standard Thompson Sampling, budgeted Thompson Sampling, and greedy HTE estimation across all budget levels tested.

</details>


### [140] [Entropy Centroids as Intrinsic Rewards for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26173)
*Wenshuo Zhao,Qi Zhu,Xingshan Zeng,Fei Mi,Lifeng Shang,Yiren Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵质心的内在奖励方法（Lowest Centroid），通过捕捉模型推理过程中熵的时序结构（高熵阶段HEP）来在多个采样响应中选择最优答案，无需外部奖励模型。在数学、代码、逻辑推理和智能体任务上，从14B到480B规模模型中均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时计算扩展方法（如Grok Heavy、Gemini Deep Think）依赖外部奖励模型进行最优响应选择，这不仅需要训练强奖励模型，还引入了额外计算开销。基于内在信号（如置信度、熵）的方法虽有探索，但噪声较大。作者希望通过利用模型推理过程中不确定性的时序结构来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 形式化定义高熵阶段（HEP）：从高熵token开始到连续低熵token出现结束的可变长度片段。2) 定义熵质心（Entropy Centroid）：受物理质心概念启发，计算所有HEP沿推理轨迹的加权平均位置。3) 提出Lowest Centroid方法：在多个候选响应中，选择熵质心最低的响应（低质心意味着早期探索后进入置信生成，通常对应更高响应质量）。

Result: 在数学、代码生成、逻辑推理和智能体任务上，从14B到480B不同规模模型上的实验表明，Lowest Centroid方法一致优于现有基线方法，且随着模型规模增大性能提升稳定。

Conclusion: 利用推理过程中熵的时序结构（HEP和熵质心）作为内在奖励是一种有效且无需外部奖励模型的选择方法，具有良好的可扩展性和跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: An effective way to scale up test-time compute of large language models is to sample multiple responses and then select the best one, as in Grok Heavy and Gemini Deep Think. Existing selection methods often rely on external reward models, which requires training a strong reward model and introduces additional computation overhead. As an alternative, previous approaches have explored intrinsic signals, such as confidence and entropy, but these signals are noisy with naive aggregation. In this work, we observe that high-entropy tokens tend to cluster into consecutive groups during inference, providing a more stable notion of model uncertainty than individual tokens. Together, these clusters reveal temporal patterns of model uncertainty throughout the inference process. Motivated by this observation, we propose to use the temporal structure of uncertainty as an intrinsic reward. To this end, we first formalize the basic unit of segment-level uncertainty as the High Entropy Phase (HEP), a variable-length segment that begins at a high-entropy token and ends when consecutive low-entropy tokens appear. We then define the Entropy Centroid, inspired by the concept of the center of mass in physics, as the weighted average position of all HEPs along the trajectory. Intuitively, a lower centroid indicates early exploration followed by confident generation, which we find often corresponds to higher response quality. Based on this insight, we propose the Lowest Centroid method, which selects the response with the lowest entropy centroid among multiple candidates. Experiments on mathematics, code generation, logical reasoning, and agentic tasks, across model scales ranging from 14B to 480B, show that Lowest Centroid consistently outperforms existing baselines and delivers stable gains as model size increases. Code is available at https://github.com/hkust-nlp/entropy-centroid.

</details>


### [141] [Efficient and Interpretable Transformer for Counterfactual Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26188)
*Panyi Dong,Zhiyu Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征相关变换器（FCorrTransformer）及其配套的反事实注意力正则化（CAR）框架，通过可解释的注意力矩阵和组不变表示学习，在表格数据上实现反事实公平性，同时保持竞争性预测性能和更低的模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险、高监管领域（如金融、保险）中，机器学习模型需要在预测性能、可解释性和监管公平性之间取得平衡。现有注意力机制和公平感知技术无法确保反事实公平。

Method: 提出FCorrTransformer，其注意力矩阵具有统计可解释性（成对特征依赖）；引入CAR框架，在注意力层面强制敏感特征的组不变表示，促进反事实公平预测而无需显式因果假设。

Result: 在不平衡分类和回归基准上，FCorrTransformer结合CAR实现了强反事实公平性，保持了竞争性预测性能，并显著降低了模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了公平理论与机器学习模型之间的关键差距，为监管敏感领域的负责任AI提供了实用框架。

Abstract: The growing reliance of machine learning models in high-stakes, highly regulated domains such as finance and insurance has created a growing tension between predictive performance, interpretability, and regulatory fairness requirements. In these settings, models are expected not only to deliver reliable predictions but also to provide transparent decision rationales and comply with strict fairness requirements. Attention-based transformers offer powerful mechanisms for modeling complex data relationships as demonstrated in various language tasks, yet their attention mechanisms alone do not ensure counterfactually fair predictions, even when combined with fairness-aware techniques. To address these limitations, we propose the Feature Correlation Transformer (FCorrTransformer), an attention-light architecture tailored for tabular data. In this design, the attention matrix admits a direct statistical interpretation as pairwise feature dependencies, enhancing both interpretability and efficiency. Leveraging this structure, we introduce Counterfactual Attention Regularization (CAR), a framework that enforces group-invariant fair representations of sensitive features at the attention level, promoting counterfactually fair predictions without relying on explicit causal assumptions. Empirical evaluations on imbalanced classification and regression benchmarks demonstrate that FCorrTransformer combined with CAR achieves strong counterfactual fairness while maintaining competitive predictive performance and substantially reducing model complexity compared with standard transformer-based baselines. Overall, this work bridges a critical gap between fairness theory and machine learning models, offering a practical framework for responsible AI in regulatory-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [142] [DORA: A Scalable Asynchronous Reinforcement Learning System for Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26256)
*Tianhao Hu,Xiangcheng Liu,Youshao Xiao,Yang Zheng,Xuan Huang,Jinrui Ding,Yufei Zhang,Tao Liang,Hongyu Zang,Quan Chen,Yueqing Sun,Wenjie Shi,Chao Zhang,Wei Wang,Qi Gu,Yerui Sun,Yucheng Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DORA系统，通过算法-系统协同设计，采用多版本流式滚动的异步范式，在不损害算法收敛性的前提下，完全消除RL训练中长尾轨迹导致的生成瓶颈，实现2-4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练中rollout阶段占50-80%的步长时间，长尾轨迹阻塞整个训练流程。现有异步训练方法无法从根本上解决长尾轨迹问题，尤其在MoE模型中更为严重，且可能偏离标准RL训练范式，阻碍模型收敛。

Method: 提出DORA系统，核心创新是多版本流式滚动（multi-version streaming rollout），同时维护多个策略版本，在完全消除气泡的同时满足算法约束（轨迹内策略一致性、数据完整性、有界陈旧度）。

Result: 在开源基准上吞吐量提升2-3倍；在数万加速器的大规模工业应用中，相比同步训练加速2-4倍；产出的LongCat-Flash-Thinking模型在复杂推理基准上达到与最先进LLM相当的性能。

Conclusion: 算法-系统协同设计的异步滚动范式能够同时实现训练效率提升和算法收敛性保证，为大规模RL后训练提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a critical paradigm for LLM post-training, yet the rollout phase -- accounting for 50--80% of total step time -- is bottlenecked by skewed generation: long-tailed trajectories indispensable for model performance block the entire training pipeline. Asynchronous training offers a natural remedy by overlapping generation with training, but introduces a fundamental tension between efficiency and algorithmic correctness. We identify three constraints in asynchronous training to preserve convergence: intra-trajectory policy consistency, data integrity, and bounded staleness. Existing approaches fail to intrinsically address the long-tailed trajectory problem, which is further exacerbated by the imbalance characteristic of Mix-of-Experts models, or deviate from the standard RL training formulation, thereby hindering model convergence. Therefore, we propose DORA (Dynamic ORchestration for Asynchronous Rollout), which addresses this challenge through algorithm-system co-design. DORA introduces multi-version streaming rollout, a novel asynchronous paradigm that maintains multiple policy versions concurrently -- simultaneously achieving full bubble elimination without compromising algorithmic constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that our DORA system achieves substantial improvements in throughput -- up to 2--3 times higher than state-of-the-art systems on open-source benchmarks -- without compromising convergence. Furthermore, in large-scale industrial applications with tens of thousands of accelerators, DORA accelerates RL training by 2--4 times compared to synchronous training across various scenarios. The resultant open-source models, LongCat-Flash-Thinking, exhibit competitive performance on complex reasoning benchmarks, matching the capability of most advanced LLMs.

</details>


### [143] [NeuroPlastic: A Plasticity-Modulated Optimizer for Biologically Inspired Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26297)
*Douglas Jiang,Yuechen Wang,Jiayi Wang,Jiaying Geng,Qinglong Wang,Feng Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroPlastic是一种受神经生物学多因子突触可塑性启发的优化器，通过自适应多信号调制机制增强梯度更新，在图像分类任务中持续优于纯梯度方法，尤其在数据有限或噪声环境下表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数深度学习优化算法仅依赖局部梯度统计进行更新，缺乏更丰富的信号调制机制。受神经生物学中多因子突触可塑性的启发，作者希望引入一种轻量级的可塑性调制层来增强传统的梯度驱动优化方法。

Method: NeuroPlastic提出一种可塑性调制优化器，通过多个交互组件（梯度统计、类活性统计和类记忆统计）动态缩放梯度更新，形成与标准深度学习训练流程兼容的轻量调制层。

Result: 在图像分类基准上持续优于纯梯度消融版本，在Fashion-MNIST和小数据 regime 中增益更为显著；在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18迁移实验中无需重新调参即保持稳定且有竞争力。

Conclusion: 多信号可塑性启发的调制机制可作为传统梯度驱动优化的有效扩展，尤其适用于学习信号有限或噪声较大的场景，为深度学习梯度方法提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Optimization algorithms are fundamental to modern deep learning, yet most widely used methods rely on update rules based primarily on local gradient statistics. We introduce NeuroPlastic, a plasticity-modulated optimizer that augments gradient-based updates with an adaptive multi-signal modulation mechanism inspired by multi-factor synaptic plasticity, a concept from neurobiology. NeuroPlastic dynamically scales gradient updates using interacting components that capture gradient, activity-like, and memory-like statistics, forming a lightweight modulation layer compatible with standard deep learning training pipelines. Across image classification benchmarks, NeuroPlastic consistently improves over a controlled gradient-only ablation, with more pronounced gains on the Fashion-MNIST benchmark and in reduced-data regimes. In transfer experiments on CIFAR-10 with ResNet-18, the method remains stable and competitive without retuning. These results suggest that multi-signal plasticity-inspired modulation can provide a useful extension to conventional gradient-driven optimization, particularly when learning signals are limited or noisy, and offer a promising direction for gradient-based methods in deep learning.

</details>


### [144] [Cheeger--Hodge Contrastive Learning for Structurally Robust Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26301)
*Mengyang Zhao,Longlong Li,Cunquan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cheeger-Hodge联合签名并通过对比学习对齐，实现鲁棒的图表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图对比学习仅依赖数据增强来定义不变性，在结构扰动下表现脆弱，需要更稳健的表示学习框架。

Method: 提出CHCL框架，构建Cheeger-Hodge联合签名（结合代数连通性λ₂的Cheeger连通性签名与1-Hodge Laplacian低频谱），并在增强视图间对齐编码器表示与该签名。

Result: 在标准基准和迁移设置下的广泛实验表明，CHCL在性能、鲁棒性和泛化能力上持续提升。

Conclusion: CHCL通过融合全局连通性与高阶结构信息的鲁棒签名，有效解决了GCL在结构扰动下的脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL) has emerged as a prominent framework for unsupervised graph representation learning. However, relying on augmentation design alone to define the invariances learned by GCL can be brittle under structural perturbations. To address this issue, we propose Cheeger--Hodge Contrastive Learning (CHCL), a framework that aligns a perturbation-stable Cheeger--Hodge joint signature across augmented views for robust graph representation learning. The proposed signature combines a Cheeger-inspired connectivity signature derived from the algebraic connectivity \(λ_2\) with the low-frequency spectrum of the 1-Hodge Laplacian, thereby capturing both global connectivity and higher-order structural information. By aligning encoder representations with the proposed Cheeger--Hodge joint signature across augmented views, CHCL learns graph embeddings that are robust to local structural perturbations. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks, transfer settings demonstrate that CHCL consistently improves performance, robustness, and generalization.

</details>


### [145] [Addressing Performance Saturation for LLM RL via Precise Entropy Curve Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26326)
*Bolian Li,Yifan Wang,Yi Ding,Anamika Lochab,Ananth Grama,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Entrocraft，一种通过拒绝采样实现自定义熵调度的方法，有效解决RL训练中的熵崩塌和性能饱和问题，使4B模型超越8B基线，pass@K提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL算法在训练大规模语言模型时存在性能饱和与熵崩塌问题，虽然已有正则化或裁剪等尝试，但长期训练中熵曲线不稳定，阻碍了性能提升。

Method: Entrocraft通过拒绝采样偏置优势分布，实现用户自定义的熵调度。该方法无需目标正则化，与优势估计器无关，理论上将每步熵变化与优势分布建立联系。

Result: 实验表明线性退火熵调度（从高熵至略低目标）效果最佳；Entrocraft使4B模型超越8B基线，训练时间延长4倍后才达到平台期，pass@K提升50%。

Conclusion: Entrocraft有效解决了RL训练中的性能饱和问题，显著提升了泛化能力、输出多样性和长期训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has unlocked complex reasoning abilities in large language models (LLMs). However, most RL algorithms suffer from performance saturation, preventing further gains as RL training scales. This problem can be characterized by the collapse of entropy, a key diagnostic for exploration in RL. Existing attempts have tried to prevent entropy collapse through regularization or clipping, but their resulting entropy curves often exhibit instability in the long term, which hinders performance gains. In this paper, we introduce Entrocraft, a simple rejection-sampling approach that realizes any user-customized entropy schedule by biasing the advantage distributions. Entrocraft requires no objective regularization and is advantage-estimator-agnostic. Theoretically, we relate per-step entropy change to the advantage distribution under minimal assumptions, which explains the behavior of existing RL and entropy-preserving methods. Entrocraft also enables a systematic study of entropy schedules, where we find that linear annealing, which starts high and decays to a slightly lower target, performs best. Empirically, Entrocraft addresses performance saturation, significantly improving generalization, output diversity, and long-term training. It enables a 4B model to outperform an 8B baseline, sustains improvement for up to 4x longer before plateauing, and raises pass@K by 50% over the baseline.

</details>


### [146] [AlphaJet: Automated Conceptual Aircraft Synthesis via Disentangled Generative Priors and Topology-Preserving Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26337)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlphaJet是一个端到端的自动化概念飞机设计流水线，能从文本任务说明实时演化出可行的3D飞机，通过可解释的潜空间、拓扑精英遗传算法和安装感知几何评分，在CPU上实现交互式设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概念飞机设计依赖人类专家的迭代循环（提出配置→运行低阶物理→检查结果→重新提出），过程耗时且依赖经验。作者希望自动化这一闭环，实现端到端的实时自动化设计。

Method: 提出三个核心贡献：(i) 解剖解耦变分自编码器(AD-VAE)，前25个潜变量受监督地对应命名解剖参数，提供可解释的形状先验；(ii) 拓扑精英遗传算法，保护五种尾翼拓扑结构中最优个体并触发停滞重启，防止过早收敛到单一构型；(iii) 安装感知几何评分，计算机身与引擎之间的符号穿透，消除生成模型中常见的冗余伪影。

Result: 完整流水线可在CPU上交互式运行，实时将每一代结果流式传输到浏览器查看器，实现实际可用的早期阶段设计空间探索工具。

Conclusion: AlphaJet将概念飞机设计从专家驱动的迭代过程转变为端到端的自动化合成，提供了可解释、可交互且计算高效的实用工具。

Abstract: Conceptual aircraft design is traditionally an expert-mediated iterative process in which a human designer proposes a configuration, runs low-order physics, inspects the result, and re-proposes. We present AlphaJet, an end-to-end automated synthesis pipeline that closes this loop. From a textual mission specification (mass, range, cruise speed, hard size envelope, engine count, areal density) AlphaJet evolves a feasible 3D aircraft in real time, scored by a transparent multi-disciplinary fitness function covering aerodynamics, structures, weights, stability, packaging, and geometric mount consistency. Three contributions distinguish our approach: (i) an Anatomically-Disentangled Variational Autoencoder (AD-VAE) whose first 25 latent dimensions are supervised to align with named anatomical parameters, providing an interpretable shape prior; (ii) a topology-elitist genetic algorithm that protects the best individual from each of five tail topologies and triggers stagnation restarts, preventing premature collapse to a single configuration; and (iii) mount-aware geometric scoring that computes signed penetration between engines and other structural parts, eliminating the redundant artifacts common in generative aircraft models. The full loop runs interactively on a CPU and streams every generation to a browser viewer, making it a practical real-world automation tool for early-phase design-space exploration.

</details>


### [147] [Uncertainty-Aware Reward Discounting for Mitigating Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26360)
*Disha Singha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双源不确定性感知奖励框架，通过集成模型不确定性（价值预测的集成分歧）和偏好不确定性（奖励标注的变异性），结合置信度调整的可靠性过滤器自适应调节动作选择，在离散网格和高维连续控制环境中实现93.7%的奖励黑客行为减少，并在高达30%的监督噪声下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL系统假设奖励函数精确可靠，但真实世界中的目标（尤其来自人类偏好）常存在不确定性、上下文依赖和内部不一致性，导致奖励黑客、过度优化和过度自信行为等对齐失败问题。

Method: 提出双源不确定性感知奖励框架：1) 通过集成模型（ensemble）在价值预测上的分歧捕获认知不确定性；2) 通过奖励标注的变异性捕获偏好不确定性；3) 结合两者通过置信度调整的可靠性过滤器（Reliability Filter）自适应调节动作选择，平衡探索与谨慎。

Result: 在6x6/8x8/10x10离散网格和Hopper-v4/Walker2d-v4连续控制环境中，实现93.7%的奖励黑客行为（陷阱访问频率）减少，训练动态更稳定，在高达30%的监督噪声下保持统计显著性，但峰值奖励相比无约束基线有所折衷。

Conclusion: 将不确定性作为奖励信号的一等公民（first-class component）处理，为构建更可靠、更对齐的强化学习系统提供了原则性方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) systems typically optimize scalar reward functions that assume precise and reliable evaluation of outcomes. However, real-world objectives--especially those derived from human preferences--are often uncertain, context-dependent, and internally inconsistent. This mismatch can lead to alignment failures such as reward hacking, over-optimization, and overconfident behavior.
  We introduce a dual-source uncertainty-aware reward framework that explicitly models both epistemic uncertainty in value estimation and uncertainty in human preferences. Model uncertainty is captured via ensemble disagreement over value predictions, while preference uncertainty is derived from variability in reward annotations. We combine these signals through a confidence-adjusted Reliability Filter that adaptively modulates action selection, encouraging a balance between exploitation and caution.
  Empirical results across multiple discrete grid configurations (6x6, 8x8, 10x10) and high-dimensional continuous control environments (Hopper-v4, Walker2d-v4) demonstrate that our approach yields more stable training dynamics and reduces exploitative behaviors under reward ambiguity, achieving a 93.7% reduction in reward-hacking behavior as measured by trap visitation frequency. We demonstrate statistical significance of these improvements and robustness under up to 30% supervisory noise, albeit with a trade-off in peak observed reward compared to unconstrained baselines.
  By treating uncertainty as a first-class component of the reward signal, this work offers a principled approach toward more reliable and aligned reinforcement learning systems.

</details>


### [148] [CoQuant: Joint Weight-Activation Subspace Projection for Mixed-Precision LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26378)
*Zhe Ding,Su Pan,Duowei Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoQuant提出了一种联合权重-激活子空间投影方法，通过理论建模输出误差并推导闭式加权PCA解，在低比特LLM量化中平衡权重和激活协方差以选择最优高精度子空间，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度后训练量化方法仅依赖激活统计量构建高精度子空间，忽略了线性操作中输出扰动由激活和权重量化噪声共同驱动这一基本性质。

Method: 通过理论建模期望输出误差，推导闭式加权PCA解来平衡权重和激活协方差，从而联合选择最优高精度子空间。

Result: 在Llama-3.2和Qwen2.5模型上，CoQuant在WikiText困惑度和零样本常识推理准确率上均一致优于强PTQ基线。

Conclusion: 联合权重-激活子空间建模为低比特LLM量化提供了一种有原则且有效的方向。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has become an important technique for reducing the inference cost of Large Language Models (LLMs). While recent mixed-precision methods improve ultra-low bit quantization by preserving critical subspaces in high precision, they typically construct these subspaces relying solely on activation statistics. This ignores the fundamental nature of linear operations, where the output perturbation is jointly driven by both activation and weight quantization noise. In this paper, we propose CoQuant, a joint weight-activation subspace projection method. By theoretically modeling the expected output error, CoQuant formulates a closed-form weighted PCA solution that balances activation and weight covariances to select the optimal high-precision subspace. Extensive experiments on Llama-3.2 and Qwen2.5 models show that CoQuant consistently outperforms strong PTQ baselines in both WikiText perplexity and zero-shot common-sense reasoning accuracy. These results demonstrate that joint weight-activation subspace modeling provides a principled and effective direction for low-bit LLM quantization. The source code is available at https://github.com/Zachary5895/CoQuant.

</details>


### [149] [Unifying Runtime Monitoring Approaches for Safety-Critical Machine Learning: Application to Vision-Based Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26411)
*Mathieu Dario,Florent Chenevier,Kévin Delmas,Joris Guerin,Jérémie Guiochet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将ML运行时监控方法分为三类：ODD监控（运行设计域）、OOD监控（分布外检测）和OMS监控（模型范围外检测），并通过航空安全关键场景（跑道检测）验证了该分类的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML运行时监控研究分散于不同社区，方法独立发展，缺乏统一分类体系，阻碍了安全关键领域中对监控方案的系统性设计与评估。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将运行时监控方法归类为ODD监控（确保运行条件符合预期）、OOD监控（拒绝偏离训练数据的输入）和OMS监控（基于模型内部状态或输出检测异常行为），并在航空着陆跑道检测任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 该分类框架有助于监控活动设计，各类监控器互补；支持使用统一的安全导向指标对不同监控器进行评估与比较。

Conclusion: 该框架为安全关键ML应用的运行时监控提供了系统化的分类体系，促进了监控方案的设计、评估与比较，提升了安全性。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring is essential to ensure the safety of ML applications in safety-critical domains. However, current research is fragmented, with independent methods emerging from different communities. In this paper, we propose a unified framework categorising runtime monitoring approaches into three distinct types: Operational Design Domain (ODD) monitoring, which ensures compliance with expected operating conditions; Out-of-Distribution (OOD) monitoring, which rejects inputs that deviate from the training data; and Out-of-Model-Scope (OMS) monitoring, which detects anomalous model behaviour based its internal states or outputs. We demonstrate the benefits of this categorization with a dedicated experiment on an aeronautical safety-critical application: runway detection during landing. This framework facilitates design of monitoring activities, with complementary categories of monitors, and enables evaluation and comparison of different monitors using common, safety-oriented metrics.

</details>


### [150] [STLGT: A Scalable Trace-Based Linear Graph Transformer for Tail Latency Prediction in Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26422)
*Yongliang Ding,Qigong Bi,Peng Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STLGT是一种基于线性图Transformer的端到端尾延迟预测器，通过将微服务调用链编码为跨度图，在保持线性推理复杂度的同时，准确预测p95尾延迟。在三个数据集上平均MAPE比PERT-GNN低8.5%，CPU推理速度提升最高12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统中精确的端到端尾延迟预测对主动SLO管理至关重要，但现有方法难以同时处理长程依赖传播、非平稳突发工作负载以及大规模推理效率问题。

Method: 提出STLGT，一种基于线性图Transformer的每API预测器。它将跟踪数据编码为跨度图，采用结构感知的线性图Transformer以与跨度图大小成线性时间的复杂度传播跨服务依赖，并结合解耦的时间模块捕捉工作负载动态。

Result: 在个性化教育微服务应用、DeathStarBench和阿里巴巴跟踪数据上，STLGT相比PERT-GNN平均MAPE降低8.5%；在N=32（阿里巴巴跟踪预处理后的最大跨度图大小）时，CPU推理速度提升最高12倍。消融实验验证了各组件在突发流量下的有效性。

Conclusion: STLGT通过结构感知线性图Transformer和解耦时间模块，实现了准确且高效的尾延迟预测，特别在突发流量场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Accurate end-to-end tail-latency forecasting is critical for proactive SLO management in microservice systems. However, modeling long-range dependency propagation and non-stationary, bursty workloads while maintaining inference efficiency at scale remains challenging. We present STLGT (Scalable Trace-based Linear Graph Transformer), a per-API predictor that encodes traces as span graphs for multi-step p95 tail-latency forecasting. STLGT uses a structure-aware linear graph Transformer to propagate cross-service dependencies with inference time linear in span graph size, and a decoupled temporal module to capture workload dynamics. Across a personalized education microservice application, DeathStarBench, and Alibaba traces, STLGT improves forecasting accuracy over PERT-GNN by 8.5% MAPE on average and achieves up to 12x faster CPU inference at N=32, matching the maximum span graph size after preprocessing the Alibaba traces. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of each component, especially under bursty traffic.

</details>


### [151] [Near-Optimal Cryptographic Hardness of Learning With Homogeneous Halfspaces Under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26446)
*Jizhou Huang,Brendan Juba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在高斯分布下识别齐次半空间的三个问题：agnostic学习、单侧可靠学习和公平性审计。在LWE假设下，证明了这些问题的近最优计算困难性结果，将先前针对一般半空间的困难性结果推广到齐次半空间，并缩小了agnostic学习齐次半空间的上界与下界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进对齐次半空间在高斯分布下相关问题的计算复杂度理解，填补一般半空间与齐次半空间之间硬度结果的空白，并缩小agnostic学习问题上界与下界之间的差距。

Method: 基于广泛接受的LWE（Learning With Errors）困难性假设，通过约简证明三个问题（agnostic学习、单侧可靠学习、公平性审计）的近最优计算下界。

Result: 证明了在LWE假设下，这三个问题的近最优计算困难性结果，将先前针对一般半空间的硬度结果推广到齐次半空间，并严格推广了先前的下界结果。

Conclusion: 齐次半空间在高斯分布下的agnostic学习、单侧可靠学习和公平性审计问题在计算上本质困难，且该困难性近乎最优，缩小了理论上下界与现有算法上界之间的差距。

Abstract: We study three problems that involve identifying homogeneous halfspaces under Gaussian distributions: agnostic learning, one-sided reliable learning, and fairness auditing. In each of these problems, we are given labeled examples $(\mathbf{x}, \mathrm{y})$ drawn from an unknown distribution on $\mathbb{R}^d\times\{-1, +1\}$, whose marginal distribution on $\mathbf{x}$ is standard Gaussian and on $\mathrm{y}$ is arbitrary. The goal of each problem is to output a homogeneous halfspace that approaches the best-fitting homogeneous halfspace in terms of its corresponding loss measure. We prove near-optimal computational hardness results for these problems under the widely believed hardness assumption of the Learning With Errors (LWE) problem. Prior hardness results for these problems were mostly established for general halfspaces; our findings extend some of these hardness results to homogeneous halfspaces. Remarkably, our lower bound strictly generalizes over prior works and narrows the gap between the upper and lower bounds for agnostically learning homogeneous halfspaces under Gaussian marginals.

</details>


### [152] [Hierarchical adaptive control for real-time dynamic inference at the edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26470)
*Francesco Daghero,Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mikkel Baun Kjærgaard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层自适应架构，用于在异构边缘节点上部署动态ML模型。全局调度器配置分类器级联，本地控制器跟踪数据漂移和硬件资源，实现延迟降低2.45倍、能耗降低2.86倍，准确率下降不到4%。


<details>
